theorem IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection_lift.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {V : Type u_3}
  (b : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock E X V) (t₀ : ZMod 2) (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates b),
  IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z :=
fun {E} {X} {V} b t₀ z =>
  IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
    b = t → IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z) b
    (fun e a d h =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
        ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates b),
          IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z)
        (fun z =>
          Eq.refl
            (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained e a d) t₀
              (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained e a d) t₀ z)))
        (Eq.symm h) z)
    (fun e p v h =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
        ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates b),
          IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z)
        (fun z =>
          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
            p = t →
              IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                  (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀ z) =
                z)
            p
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p =>
                ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v)),
                  IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                      (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                        z) =
                    z)
                (fun z => of_eq_true (eq_self z)) (Eq.symm h) z)
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p =>
                ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v)),
                  IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                      (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                        z) =
                    z)
                (fun z => of_eq_true (eq_self z)) (Eq.symm h) z)
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p =>
                ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v)),
                  IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                      (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                        z) =
                    z)
                (fun z =>
                  of_eq_true
                    (Eq.trans
                      (congrArg (fun x => x = z)
                        (Eq.trans
                          (congr
                            (congrArg HAdd.hAdd
                              (Eq.trans
                                (Eq.trans
                                  (congrArg (fun x => (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0) + (x, 0).1)
                                    (sub_eq_add_neg z (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0)))
                                  (add_left_comm (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0) z
                                    (-if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0)))
                                (Eq.trans (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd z) (add_neg_cancel (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0)))
                                  (add_zero z))))
                            (congrArg (fun x => (x, 0).2) (sub_eq_add_neg z (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0))))
                          (add_zero z)))
                      (eq_self z)))
                (Eq.symm h) z)
            (Eq.refl p))
        (Eq.symm h) z)
    (fun e h =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
        ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates b),
          IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z)
        (fun z =>
          PUnit.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
            z = t →
              IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e) t₀
                  (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e) t₀ z) =
                z)
            z
            (fun h =>
              Eq.symm h ▸
                Eq.refl
                  (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e) t₀
                    (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e) t₀
                      PUnit.unit)))
            (Eq.refl z))
        (Eq.symm h) z)
    (Eq.refl b)

theorem IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection_preserves_homogeneous.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} {k : ℕ} (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X V) (p : ZMod 2 × (Fin k → ZMod 2 × ZMod 2)),
  (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection t p).1 = p.1 :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} t p => rfl

theorem IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection_surjective.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} {k : ℕ} (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X V),
  Function.Surjective (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection t) :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} t b =>
  Prod.casesOn b fun t₀ z =>
    Exists.intro (t₀, fun i => IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (t.blocks i) t₀ (z i))
      (Prod.ext
        (Eq.refl
          (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection t
              (t₀, fun i => IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (t.blocks i) t₀ (z i))).1)
        (funext fun i => IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection_lift (t.blocks i) t₀ (z i)))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.source_table_pullback_evaluation.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} {k : ℕ} [inst : AddCommMonoid V] [inst_1 : Module (ZMod 2) V]
  (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X V) (p : ZMod 2 × (Fin k → ZMod 2 × ZMod 2)),
  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullTable (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable t) p =
    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedTable t (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection t p) :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} [AddCommMonoid V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] t p =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congr
        (congrArg
          (fun x => Eq (p.1 • (t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i)) + x))
          (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
            Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x))))
        (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (p.1 • t.intercept))
          (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
            Eq.refl
              (IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)
                (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (t.blocks x) p.1 (p.2 x)))))))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            _a +
                ∑ x : Fin k,
                  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x) =
              p.1 • t.intercept +
                ∑ x : Fin k,
                  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)
                    (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (t.blocks x) p.1 (p.2 x)))
          (smul_add p.1 t.intercept (∑ i : Fin k, IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i)))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              p.1 • t.intercept + _a +
                  ∑ x : Fin k,
                    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x) =
                p.1 • t.intercept +
                  ∑ x : Fin k,
                    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)

        Trans.trans

                        (Lean.Data.AC.Context.eq_of_norm

                            vars :=
                              [{ value := x, neutral := none }, { value := x_1, neutral := none },
                                { value := x_2, neutral := none }],
                            arbitrary := x }
                          (((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0)).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1))
                          ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0).op ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2)))
                          (Eq.refl true)))
                    (∑ x : Fin k, p.1 • IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks x))
                    (∑ x : Fin k,
                      IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x))
                    (p.1 • t.intercept)))
                ((fun x x_1 x_2 =>
                    id
                      (Lean.Data.AC.Context.eq_of_norm
                        { op := HAdd.hAdd, assoc := AddSemigroup.to_isAssociative,

                        ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2).op ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0)))

                (add_zero 0)))
            (eq_self 0)))
    (Eq.refl b)

theorem IndependentDuodenary.pullback_reduced_intercept.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {V : Type u_3}
  {k : ℕ} [inst : AddCommMonoid V] [inst_1 : DecidableEq V],
  (∀ (v : V), v + v = 0) →
    ∀ (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X V),
      IndependentDuodenary.fullReducedIntercept (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable t) =
        IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} [AddCommMonoid V] [DecidableEq V] hDouble t =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun x =>
          t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i) + x =
            t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).2)
        (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
          Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x))).2)))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg (fun _a => _a = t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).2)
          (add_assoc t.intercept (∑ i : Fin k, IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i))
            (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x))).2))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              t.intercept + _a = t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).2)
            (Eq.symm Finset.sum_add_distrib)))
        ((fun {α β γ} [HAdd α β γ] a a_1 e_a =>
            Eq.rec (motive := fun a_2 e_a => ∀ (a_3 a_4 : β), a_3 = a_4 → a + a_3 = a_2 + a_4)
              (fun a_2 a_3 e_a => e_a ▸ Eq.refl (a + a_2)) e_a)
          t.intercept t.intercept (Eq.refl t.intercept)
          (∑ x : Fin k,
            (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks x) +
              (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x))).2))
          (∑ i : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).2)
          (Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i a => IndependentDuodenary.pullback_block_intercept hDouble (t.blocks i)))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.permitted_pullback_exact_key.{u_1, u_2, u_4, u_5} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {k : ℕ}
  {K : Type u_4} {W : Type u_5} [inst : AddCommMonoid K] [inst_1 : AddCommMonoid W] [inst_2 : DecidableEq K]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq W],
  (∀ (v : K × W), v + v = 0) →
    ∀ (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X (K × W)),
      IndependentDuodenary.fullExactKey (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable t) =
        IndependentDuodenary.projectedExactKey t :=
fun {E} {X} {k} {K} {W} [AddCommMonoid K] [AddCommMonoid W] [DecidableEq K] [DecidableEq W] hDouble t =>
  let_fun hi := IndependentDuodenary.pullback_reduced_intercept hDouble t;
  let_fun hm := funext fun i => IndependentDuodenary.pullback_block_metadata (t.blocks i);
  id
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            {
                metadata := fun i =>
                  (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock ((IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable t).blocks i)).1,
                binaryIntercept := _a.1, complementIntercept := _a.2 } =
              { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1,
                binaryIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).1,
                complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).2 })
          hi))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              { metadata := _a, binaryIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).1,
                  complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).2 } =
                { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1,
                  binaryIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).1,
                  complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).2 })
            hm))
        (Eq.refl
          { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1,
            binaryIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).1,
            complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).2 })))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.projected_exact_key_translate.{u_1, u_2, u_4, u_5} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {k : ℕ} {K : Type u_4} {W : Type u_5} [inst : AddCommMonoid K] [inst_1 : AddCommMonoid W] [inst_2 : DecidableEq K]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq W] (h : K) (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X (K × W)),
  IndependentDuodenary.projectedExactKey (IndependentDuodenary.translateProjectedTable h t) =
    IndependentDuodenary.translateKey h (IndependentDuodenary.projectedExactKey t) :=
fun {E} {X} {k} {K} {W} [AddCommMonoid K] [AddCommMonoid W] [DecidableEq K] [DecidableEq W] h t =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congr
        (congrArg Eq
          (congr
            (congrArg
              (IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.mk fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1)
              (Eq.trans
                (congrArg Prod.fst
                  (Eq.trans
                    (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (t.intercept + (h, 0)))
                      (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                        Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2))
                    (add_assoc t.intercept (h, 0)
                      (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2))))
                (add_left_comm t.intercept.1 h
                  (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).1)))
            (Eq.trans
              (congrArg Prod.snd
                (Eq.trans
                  (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (t.intercept + (h, 0)))
                    (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                      Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2))
                  (add_assoc t.intercept (h, 0)
                    (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2))))
              (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd t.intercept.2)
                (zero_add (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).2)))))
        (congrArg
          (fun x =>
            { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1, binaryIntercept := x,
              complementIntercept :=
                t.intercept.2 + (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).2 })
          (add_comm (t.intercept.1 + (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).1) h)))
      (eq_self
        { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1,
          binaryIntercept :=
            h + (t.intercept.1 + (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).1),
          complementIntercept :=
            t.intercept.2 + (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).2 }))

            { metadata := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedExactKey t).metadata, binaryIntercept := 0,
              complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedExactKey t).complementIntercept }))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.split_binary_double_zero : ∀ {ℓ d : ℕ} (v : (Fin ℓ → ZMod 2) × (Fin d → ZMod 2)),
  v + v = 0 :=

      (eq_self (some p)))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.encode_parsed_station_parameter : ∀ (c : IndependentDuodenary.StationWireCode)
  (p : IndependentDuodenary.AffineStationParameter),
  IndependentDuodenary.parseStationParameter c = some p → IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter p = c :=
fun c p h =>
  Prod.casesOn (motive := fun x =>
    IndependentDuodenary.parseStationParameter x = some p → IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter p = x) c
    (fun x b h =>
      Option.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
        IndependentDuodenary.parseStationUnit x = t → IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter p = (x, b))
        (IndependentDuodenary.parseStationUnit x)
        (fun h_1 =>
          Eq.ndrec (motive := fun x_1 =>
            IndependentDuodenary.parseStationUnit x = x_1 → IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter p = (x, b))
            (fun hp =>
              False.elim
                (Eq.mp
                  (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => Option.map (fun u => (u, b)) x = some p) hp)
                    (eq_false' fun h => Option.noConfusion h))
                  h))
            (Eq.symm h_1) (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.parseStationUnit x)))
        (fun u h_1 =>
          Eq.ndrec (motive := fun x_1 =>
            IndependentDuodenary.parseStationUnit x = x_1 → IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter p = (x, b))
            (fun hp =>
              let_fun heq :=
                Eq.mp
                  (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => Option.map (fun u => (u, b)) x = some p) hp)
                    (Option.some.injEq (u, b) p))
                  h;
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p =>
                IndependentDuodenary.parseStationParameter (x, b) = some p →
                  IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter p = (x, b))
                (fun h => Prod.ext (IndependentDuodenary.parse_station_unit_sound x u hp) rfl) heq h)
            (Eq.symm h_1) (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.parseStationUnit x)))
        (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.parseStationUnit x)))
    h

theorem IndependentDuodenary.station_wire_composition : ∀ (outer inner : IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr),
  IndependentDuodenary.composeStationWire (IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationExpression outer)
      (IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationExpression inner) =
    some (IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationExpression (outer.compose inner)) :=
fun outer inner =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congrArg
        (fun x =>
          x =
            some
              (IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter
                (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationCompose (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation outer)
                  (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation inner))))
        (congr
          (congrArg bind
            (IndependentDuodenary.parse_station_parameter_roundtrip (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation outer)))
          (funext fun p =>
            congrArg
              (fun x => do
                let q ← x
                some (IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationCompose p q)))
              (IndependentDuodenary.parse_station_parameter_roundtrip (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation inner)))))
      (eq_self
        (some
          (IndependentDuodenary.encodeStationParameter
            (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationCompose (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation outer)
              (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation inner))))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.source_shift_expression_add.{u_1} : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : AddMonoid K] (χ : K →+ ZMod 2)
  (h g : K),
  IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning (IndependentDuodenary.sourceShiftExpression χ (h + g)) =
    IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning
      ((IndependentDuodenary.sourceShiftExpression χ h).compose (IndependentDuodenary.sourceShiftExpression χ g)) :=
fun {K} [AddMonoid K] χ h g =>
  (IndependentDuodenary.station_meaning_iff_compiled (IndependentDuodenary.sourceShiftExpression χ (h + g))
        ((IndependentDuodenary.sourceShiftExpression χ h).compose (IndependentDuodenary.sourceShiftExpression χ g))).mpr
    (of_eq_true
      (Eq.trans
        (congr (congrArg (fun x => Eq (1, x)) (map_add (IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) h g))
          (congr (congrArg Prod.mk (mul_one 1))
            (Eq.trans
              (congrArg (fun x => x + (IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) h)
                (one_mul ((IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) g)))
              (add_comm ((IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) g) ((IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) h)))))
        (eq_self (1, (IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) h + (IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) g))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.affine_permutation_inverse : ∀ (p : IndependentDuodenary.AffineStationParameter),
  IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationInverse p) =
    (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation p)⁻¹ :=
fun p =>
  Equiv.Perm.ext fun x =>
    let_fun this :=
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congr
            (congrArg Eq
              (congr (congrArg (fun x_1 => HAdd.hAdd (↑(p.1⁻¹, x_1).1 * x)) (neg_mul (↑p.1⁻¹) p.2))
                (congrArg (fun x => (p.1⁻¹, x).2) (neg_mul (↑p.1⁻¹) p.2))))
            (Eq.trans (Eq.trans (congrArg (HMul.hMul ↑p.1⁻¹) (sub_eq_add_neg x p.2)) (mul_add (↑p.1⁻¹) x (-p.2)))
              (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (↑p.1⁻¹ * x)) (mul_neg (↑p.1⁻¹) p.2))))
          (eq_self (↑p.1⁻¹ * x + -(↑p.1⁻¹ * p.2))));
    this

theorem IndependentDuodenary.station_compile_correct : ∀ (e : IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr),
  IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation e) =
    IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e :=
fun e =>
  IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.rec
    (Equiv.Perm.ext fun x =>
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrArg (fun x_1 => x_1 = x)
            (Eq.trans
              (congrArg (fun x_1 => x_1 x)
                ((fun {α β} toFun toFun_1 e_toFun =>
                    Eq.rec (motive := fun toFun_2 e_toFun =>
                      ∀ (invFun invFun_1 : β → α) (e_invFun : invFun = invFun_1)
                        (left_inv : Function.LeftInverse invFun toFun) (right_inv : Function.RightInverse invFun toFun),
                        { toFun := toFun, invFun := invFun, left_inv := left_inv, right_inv := right_inv } =
                          { toFun := toFun_2, invFun := invFun_1, left_inv := e_toFun ▸ e_invFun ▸ left_inv,
                            right_inv := e_toFun ▸ e_invFun ▸ right_inv })
                      (fun invFun invFun_1 e_invFun =>
                        Eq.rec (motive := fun invFun_2 e_invFun =>
                          ∀ (left_inv : Function.LeftInverse invFun toFun)
                            (right_inv : Function.RightInverse invFun toFun),
                            { toFun := toFun, invFun := invFun, left_inv := left_inv, right_inv := right_inv } =
                              { toFun := toFun, invFun := invFun_2, left_inv := Eq.refl toFun ▸ e_invFun ▸ left_inv,
                                right_inv := Eq.refl toFun ▸ e_invFun ▸ right_inv })
                          (fun left_inv right_inv =>
                            Eq.refl { toFun := toFun, invFun := invFun, left_inv := left_inv, right_inv := right_inv })
                          e_invFun)
                      e_toFun)
                  (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationApply
                    (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.identity))
                  (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationApply (1, 0))
                  (id (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationApply (1, 0))))
                  (fun x =>
                    ↑(IndependentDuodenary.compileStation IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.identity).1⁻¹ *
                      (x - (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.identity).2))
                  (fun x => x)
                  (funext fun x => Eq.trans (congr (congrArg (fun x => HMul.hMul ↑x) inv_one) (sub_zero x)) (one_mul x))
                  (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation.proof_1
                    (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.identity))
                  (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation.proof_2
                    (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.identity))))
              (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x + 0) (one_mul x)) (add_zero x))))
          (eq_self x)))
    (fun k =>
      Eq.refl
        (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation
          (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation (IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.shift k))))
    (Eq.refl
      (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation
        (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.reflect)))
    (fun a =>
      Eq.refl
        (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation
          (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation (IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr.scale a))))
    (fun outer inner ho hi =>
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrArg (fun x => x = IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning outer * IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning inner)
            (Eq.trans
              (IndependentDuodenary.affine_permutation_compose (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation outer)
                (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation inner))
              (congr (congrArg HMul.hMul ho) hi)))
          (eq_self (IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning outer * IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning inner))))
    (fun body h =>
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrArg (fun x => x = (IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning body)⁻¹)
            (Eq.trans (IndependentDuodenary.affine_permutation_inverse (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation body))
              (congrArg Inv.inv h)))
          (eq_self (IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning body)⁻¹)))
    e

theorem IndependentDuodenary.compile_parameter_expression : ∀ (p : IndependentDuodenary.AffineStationParameter),
  IndependentDuodenary.compileStation (IndependentDuodenary.expressionFromParameter p) = p :=
fun p =>
  Prod.casesOn p fun a b =>
    of_eq_true
      (Eq.trans
        (congrArg (fun x => x = (a, b))
          (congr (congrArg Prod.mk (one_mul a)) (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x + b) (mul_zero 1)) (zero_add b))))
        (eq_self (a, b)))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.station_normalization_meaning : ∀ (e : IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr),
  IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning (IndependentDuodenary.normalizeStation e) =
    IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e :=
fun e =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg (fun _a => _a = IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e)
        (Eq.symm (IndependentDuodenary.station_compile_correct (IndependentDuodenary.normalizeStation e)))))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation _a) =
              IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e)
          (IndependentDuodenary.normalizeStation.eq_1 e)))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg (fun _a => IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation _a = IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e)
            (IndependentDuodenary.compile_parameter_expression (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation e))))
        (Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg (fun _a => _a = IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e)
              (IndependentDuodenary.station_compile_correct e)))
          (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e)))))

  IndependentDuodenary.normalizeStation (IndependentDuodenary.normalizeStation e) =
    IndependentDuodenary.normalizeStation e :=

  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congrArg
        (fun x =>
          IndependentDuodenary.expressionFromParameter x =
            IndependentDuodenary.expressionFromParameter (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation e))
        (IndependentDuodenary.compile_parameter_expression (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation e)))
      (eq_self (IndependentDuodenary.expressionFromParameter (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation e))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.station_meaning_iff_compiled : ∀ (e f : IndependentDuodenary.StationExpr),
  IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e = IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning f ↔
    IndependentDuodenary.compileStation e = IndependentDuodenary.compileStation f :=
fun e f =>
  {
    mp := fun h =>
      IndependentDuodenary.affine_station_permutation_injective
        (Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a => _a = IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation f))
              (IndependentDuodenary.station_compile_correct e)))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg (fun _a => IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e = _a)
                (IndependentDuodenary.station_compile_correct f)))
            (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning f) h))
              (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning f))))),
    mpr := fun h =>
      Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg (fun _a => _a = IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning f)
            (Eq.symm (IndependentDuodenary.station_compile_correct e))))
        (Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a => IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation e) = _a)
              (Eq.symm (IndependentDuodenary.station_compile_correct f))))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg
                (fun _a =>
                  IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation _a =
                    IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation f))
                h))
            (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.affineStationPermutation (IndependentDuodenary.compileStation f))))) }
'IndependentDuodenary.station_meaning_iff_compiled' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
DIRECT DEPENDENCIES station_meaning_iff_compiled: [IndependentDuodenary.affine_station_permutation_injective,
 Eq.mpr,
 congrArg,
 id,
 instOfNatNat,

  IndependentDuodenary.sameStationMeaning e f = true ↔
    IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning e = IndependentDuodenary.stationMeaning f :=

  IndependentDuodenary.reflectStation (IndependentDuodenary.rotateStation (IndependentDuodenary.reflectStation x)) =
    x - 1 :=

  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congr
        (congrArg Eq
          (Eq.trans (neg_add_rev (-x) 1) (Eq.trans (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (-1)) (neg_neg x)) (add_comm (-1) x))))

      (eq_self (x + -1)))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.cyclic_distance_translation : ∀ (a b k : ZMod 12),
  IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (a + k) (b + k) = IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance a b :=
fun a b k =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congrArg (fun x => x = (b - a).val ⊓ (12 - (b - a).val))
        (congr (congrArg (fun x => min x.val) (add_sub_add_right_eq_sub b a k))
          (congrArg (fun x => 12 - x.val) (add_sub_add_right_eq_sub b a k))))
      (eq_self ((b - a).val ⊓ (12 - (b - a).val))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_action_mul : ∀ (g h : DihedralGroup 12) (x : ZMod 12),
  IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction (g * h) x =
    IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction h x) :=
fun g h x =>
  DihedralGroup.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
    g = t →
      IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction (g * h) x =
        IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction h x))
    g
    (fun a h_1 =>
      Eq.symm h_1 ▸
        DihedralGroup.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
          h = t →
            IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction (DihedralGroup.r a * h) x =
              IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction (DihedralGroup.r a)
                (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction h x))
          h
          (fun a_1 h_2 =>
            Eq.symm h_2 ▸
              id
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf x)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a) (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a_1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add
                        (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf x) (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a_1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_gt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_zero (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))))
          (fun a_1 h_2 =>
            Eq.symm h_2 ▸
              Eq.mpr
                (id
                  (congr
                    (congrArg Eq (Eq.trans (neg_add_rev x (a_1 - a)) (congrArg (fun x_1 => x_1 + -x) (neg_sub a_1 a))))
                    (congrArg (fun x => x + a) (neg_add_rev x a_1))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.sub_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a) (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a_1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.sub_pf
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul a_1 (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw (ZMod 12) (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf x)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul x (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw (ZMod 12) (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a_1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul a_1 (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw (ZMod 12) (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf x)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul x (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw (ZMod 12) (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_gt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_zero
                        (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast +
                          (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))))
          (Eq.refl h))
    (fun a h_1 =>
      Eq.symm h_1 ▸
        DihedralGroup.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
          h = t →
            IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction (DihedralGroup.sr a * h) x =
              IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction (DihedralGroup.sr a)
                (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction h x))
          h
          (fun a_1 h_2 =>
            Eq.symm h_2 ▸
              Eq.mpr
                (id
                  (congr
                    (congrArg Eq
                      (Eq.trans (neg_add_rev x (a + a_1)) (congrArg (fun x_1 => x_1 + -x) (neg_add_rev a a_1))))
                    (Eq.trans (neg_add_rev (x + a_1) a) (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (-a)) (neg_add_rev x a_1)))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a_1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul a_1 (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw (ZMod 12) (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw (ZMod 12) (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf x)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul x (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw (ZMod 12) (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)

                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a_1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul a_1 (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)

                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf x)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul x (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)

                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))

                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_gt (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))))
          (fun a_1 h_2 =>
            Eq.symm h_2 ▸
              Eq.mpr
                (id
                  (congrArg (Eq (x + (a_1 - a)))

                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.sub_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a_1) (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.sub_pf
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul

                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)

                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))

                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add
                        (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + (a ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_mul a (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_one_mul
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)

                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw (ZMod 12) 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.neg_zero))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_congr (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf x) (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.atom_pf a_1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_add_lt (x ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.add_pf_zero_add (a_1 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))

          (Eq.refl h))
    (Eq.refl g)

theorem IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_zero_one_separates : ∀ (g h : DihedralGroup 12),
  IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g 0 = IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction h 0 →
    IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g 1 = IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction h 1 → g = h :=
of_decide_eq_true (id (Eq.refl true))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_preserves_cyclic_distance : ∀ (g : DihedralGroup 12) (a b : ZMod 12),
  IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g a)
      (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g b) =
    IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance a b :=
fun g a b =>
  DihedralGroup.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
    g = t →
      IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g a)
          (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g b) =
        IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance a b)
    g (fun k h => Eq.symm h ▸ IndependentDuodenary.cyclic_distance_translation a b k)
    (fun k h =>
      Eq.symm h ▸
        Eq.trans (IndependentDuodenary.cyclic_distance_reflection (a + k) (b + k))
          (IndependentDuodenary.cyclic_distance_translation a b k))
    (Eq.refl g)

theorem IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_matches_directed_edge : ∀ (a b : ZMod 12),
  IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance a b = 1 →
    ∃ g, IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g 0 = a ∧ IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g 1 = b :=
fun a b hab =>
  Or.casesOn ((IndependentDuodenary.cyclic_distance_one_iff a b).mp hab)
    (fun hb =>
      Exists.intro (DihedralGroup.r a)
        ⟨of_eq_true (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = a) (Eq.trans (add_comm 0 a) (add_zero a))) (eq_self a)),
          of_eq_true (Eq.trans (congr (congrArg Eq (add_comm 1 a)) hb) (eq_self (a + 1)))⟩)
    fun hb =>
    Exists.intro (DihedralGroup.sr (-a))
      ⟨of_eq_true
          (Eq.trans
            (congrArg (fun x => x = a)
              (Eq.trans (congrArg Neg.neg (Eq.trans (add_comm 0 (-a)) (add_zero (-a)))) (neg_neg a)))
            (eq_self a)),
        of_eq_true
          (Eq.trans
            (congr
              (congrArg Eq
                (Eq.trans (Eq.trans (congrArg Neg.neg (add_comm 1 (-a))) (neg_add_rev (-a) 1))
                  (Eq.trans (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (-1)) (neg_neg a)) (add_comm (-1) a))))
              (Eq.trans hb (sub_eq_add_neg a 1)))
            (eq_self (a + -1)))⟩

theorem IndependentDuodenary.cyclic_isometry_is_dihedral : ∀ (f : ZMod 12 → ZMod 12),
  (∀ (a b : ZMod 12), IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f a) (f b) = IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance a b) →
    ∃ g, f = IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g :=
fun f hf =>
  let_fun hedge := Eq.trans (hf 0 1) (of_decide_eq_true (id (Eq.refl true)));
  Exists.casesOn (IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_matches_directed_edge (f 0) (f 1) hedge) fun g h =>
    And.casesOn h fun hg0 hg1 =>
      Exists.intro g
        (funext fun x =>
          let y := IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g⁻¹ (f x);
          let_fun hinv :=
            id
              (Eq.mpr
                (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = f x) (Eq.symm (IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_action_mul g g⁻¹ (f x)))))
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id
                    (congrArg (fun _a => IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction _a (f x) = f x) (mul_inv_cancel g)))
                  (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = f x) (IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_action_one (f x))))
                    (Eq.refl (f x)))));
          let_fun hzero :=
            Trans.trans
              (Trans.trans (Eq.symm (IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_preserves_cyclic_distance g 0 y))
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id
                    (congrArg
                      (fun _a =>
                        IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance _a (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g y) =
                          IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f 0) (f x))
                      hg0))
                  (Eq.mpr
                    (id
                      (congrArg
                        (fun _a =>
                          IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f 0) _a =
                            IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f 0) (f x))
                        hinv))
                    (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f 0) (f x))))))
              (hf 0 x);
          let_fun hone :=
            Trans.trans
              (Trans.trans (Eq.symm (IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_preserves_cyclic_distance g 1 y))
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id
                    (congrArg
                      (fun _a =>
                        IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance _a (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g y) =
                          IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f 1) (f x))
                      hg1))
                  (Eq.mpr
                    (id
                      (congrArg
                        (fun _a =>
                          IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f 1) _a =
                            IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f 1) (f x))
                        hinv))
                    (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.cyclicDistance (f 1) (f x))))))
              (hf 1 x);
          let_fun hy := IndependentDuodenary.cyclic_zero_one_separates y x hzero hone;
          Eq.trans (Eq.symm hinv) (congrArg (IndependentDuodenary.dihedralStationAction g) hy))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.cyclic_station_isometry_card : Nat.card IndependentDuodenary.CyclicStationIsometry = 24 :=
Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 24) (Eq.symm (Nat.card_congr IndependentDuodenary.dihedralCyclicIsometryEquiv))))
  (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 24) Nat.card_eq_fintype_card))
    (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 24) IndependentDuodenary.dihedral_twelve_card)) (Eq.refl 24)))
